# N 開頭詞條

> 本檔收錄 2,203 條含中文對譯的詞目。

<a id="entry-n-000001"></a>
## n

*n.* 1. **氮** — a common nonmetallic element that is normally a colorless odorless tasteless inert diatomic gas; constitutes 78 percent of the atmosphere by volume; a constituent of all living tissues
2. **北, 北方** — the cardinal compass point that is at 0 or 360 degrees
3. **牛頓** — a unit of force equal to the force that imparts an acceleration of 1 m/sec/sec to a mass of 1 kilogram; equal to 100,000 dynes
4. **當量濃度, 規定濃度, 規度** — (of a solution) concentration expressed in gram equivalents of solute per liter
5. the 14th letter of the Roman alphabet

<a id="entry-n-000002"></a>
## N'Djamena

**發音：** /ən.dʒɑːˈmeɪ.nɑː/
*prop. n.* 1. **恩賈梅納 /恩賈梅納** — The capital city of Chad.

<a id="entry-n-000003"></a>
## n-dimensional

*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **N維的 /N維的** — Having an arbitrary number of dimensions.

<a id="entry-n-000004"></a>
## n-th

*adj.* 1. **最後一個** — last or greatest in an indefinitely large series
*“to the nth degree”*

<a id="entry-n-000005"></a>
## n/a

*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **不適用 /不適用** — not applicable
2. *(not-comparable)* **不可用** — not available

<a id="entry-n-000006"></a>
## na

*n.* 1. **鈉** — a silvery soft waxy metallic element of the alkali metal group; occurs abundantly in natural compounds (especially in salt water); burns with a yellow flame and reacts violently in water; occurs in sea water and in the mineral halite (rock salt)

<a id="entry-n-000007"></a>
## naan

**發音：** /nɑːn/
*n.* 1. **饢 /饢** — A type of round, flat bread baked in a tandoor popular in South and Central Asian cuisine.
2. leavened bread baked in a clay oven in India; usually shaped like a teardrop

<a id="entry-n-000008"></a>
## nab

**發音：** /næb/
*v.* 1. **當場逮捕, 抓住, 抓獲, 拘押, 拘捕** — take into custody
*“the police nabbed the suspected criminals”*
2. *(informal, transitive)* **逮捕** — To seize, arrest or take into custody (a criminal or fugitive).
3. tag the base runner to get him out
4. seize suddenly

## nadir

**發音：** /ˈneɪˌdɪə(ɹ)/
*n.* 1. **情緒的最低點, 最不幸的時刻, 最低點, 最底點** — an extreme state of adversity; the lowest point of anything
2. **天底** — The point of the celestial sphere, directly opposite the zenith; inferior pole of the horizon; point of the celestial sphere directly under the place of observation.
3. *(figuratively)* **低谷** — The lowest point; time of greatest depression.
4. the point below the observer that is directly opposite the zenith on the imaginary sphere against which celestial bodies appear to be projected

## nag

**發音：** /ˈnæɡ/
*v.* 1. *(ambitransitive)* **嘮叨 /嘮叨** — To repeatedly remind or complain to (someone) in an annoying way, often about insignificant or unnecessary matters.
2. bother persistently with trivial complaints
*“She nags her husband all day long”*
3. worry persistently
*“nagging concerns and doubts”*
4. remind or urge constantly
*“she nagged to take a vacation”*
*n.* 1. **老馬** — an old or over-worked horse
2. someone (especially a woman) who annoys people by constantly finding fault

## naif

*n.* 1. a naive or inexperienced person
*adj.* 1. **天真+的, 幼稚的** — marked by or showing unaffected simplicity and lack of guile or worldly experience
*“a teenager's naive ignorance of life”*
*“the naive assumption that things can only get better”*

<a id="entry-n-000050"></a>
## nail

*n.* 1. **手指甲, 指甲, 腳趾甲** — horny plate covering and protecting part of the dorsal surface of the digits
2. **釘子** — a thin pointed piece of metal that is hammered into materials as a fastener
3. **納爾** — a former unit of length for cloth equal to 1/16 of a yard
*v.* 1. **當場逮捕, 抓住, 抓獲, 拘押, 拘捕** — take into custody
*“the police nabbed the suspected criminals”*
2. **得到, 贏得** — succeed in obtaining a position
*“He nailed down a spot at Harvard”*
3. **輕快地通過, 輕易地通過, 輕鬆地完成, 順利通過** — succeed at easily
*“She sailed through her exams”*
*“You will pass with flying colors”*
4. **準確地轟炸, 精確地找到** — locate exactly
*“can you pinpoint the position of the enemy?”*
*“The chemists could not nail the identity of the chromosome”*
5. attach something somewhere by means of nails
*“nail the board onto the wall”*
6. hit hard
*“He smashed a 3-run homer”*

## nail clippers

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-only)* **指甲剪, 指甲刀** — A mechanical device used to trim fingernails and toenails.

<a id="entry-n-000052"></a>
## nail down

*v.* 1. **得到, 贏得** — succeed in obtaining a position
*“He nailed down a spot at Harvard”*
2. define clearly
*“I cannot narrow down the rules for this game”*
3. make final; put the last touches on; put into final form
*“let's finalize the proposal”*

<a id="entry-n-000065"></a>
## naive

**發音：** /naɪˈiːv/
*adj.* 1. **天真+的, 幼稚的** — marked by or showing unaffected simplicity and lack of guile or worldly experience
*“a teenager's naive ignorance of life”*
*“the naive assumption that things can only get better”*
2. **天真, 幼稚** — Lacking worldly experience, wisdom, or judgement; unsophisticated.
3. of or created by one without formal training; simple or naive in style
*“primitive art such as that by Grandma Moses is often colorful and striking”*
4. inexperienced
5. lacking information or instruction
*“lamentably unenlightened as to the laws”*
6. not initiated; deficient in relevant experience
*“it seemed a bizarre ceremony to uninitiated western eyes”*
*“he took part in the experiment as a naive subject”*

<a id="entry-n-000066"></a>
## naively

*adv.* 1. **天真爛漫+地, 無邪+地, 樸實+地, 樸素+地, 質樸+地** — in a naive manner
*“he believed, naively, that she would leave him her money”*

<a id="entry-n-000067"></a>
## naiveness

*n.* 1. *(form-of, rare, uncountable)* **天真** — Rare form of naïveté.
2. lack of sophistication or worldliness

<a id="entry-n-000076"></a>
## naked

**發音：** /ˈneɪkɪd/
*adj.* 1. **只用耳朵的, 肉眼的, 裸眼的** — (of the eye or ear e.g.) without the aid of an optical or acoustical device or instrument
*“visible to the naked eye”*
2. **赤裸, 裸, 裸體 /裸體** — Bare, not covered by clothing.
3. completely unclothed
*“bare bodies”*
*“naked from the waist up”*
4. having no protecting or concealing cover
*“naked to mine enemies"- Shakespeare”*
5. devoid of elaboration or diminution or concealment; bare and pure
*“naked ambition”*
*“raw fury”*
6. lacking any cover
*“naked branches of the trees”*
*“lie on the naked rock”*

<a id="entry-n-000077"></a>
## naked ape

*n.* 1. *(humorous, idiomatic)* **裸猿** — A human being.

<a id="entry-n-000078"></a>
## naked eye

**發音：** /ˌneɪ.kəˈdaɪ/
*n.* 1. **肉眼, 裸眼** — Eyesight, unaided by equipment such as a telescope or microscope.
2. the eye unaided by any optical instrument that alters the power of vision or alters the apparent size or distance of objects
*“it is not safe to look directly at the sun with the naked eye”*

<a id="entry-n-000079"></a>
## naked lady

*n.* 1. **秋水仙** — bulbous autumn-flowering herb with white, purple or lavender-and-white flowers; native to western and central Europe
2. amaryllis of South Africa often cultivated for its fragrant white or rose flowers

<a id="entry-n-000080"></a>
## naked mole rat

*n.* 1. **裸鼴鼠** — fetal-looking colonial rodent of East Africa; neither mole nor rat; they feed on tubers and have a social structure similar to that of honeybees and termites

<a id="entry-n-000081"></a>
## naked oat

*n.* 1. **莜麥 /莜麥** — Avena nuda, a grass with edible seeds.

<a id="entry-n-000082"></a>
## nakedly

*adv.* 1. **裸體+地, 赤裸裸, 露出+地** — without clothing
*“Henriette saw the weaving figure of an Apache warrior reel nakedly on a pony and rush by with a rifle raised”*
2. in an exposed manner; without protection or defense
*“they were attacked as they huddled nakedly on the bare hill”*

<a id="entry-n-000083"></a>
## nakedness

*n.* 1. **裸體, 赤裸, 赤裸裸** — the state of being without clothing or covering of any kind
2. *(uncountable, usually)* **裸體 /裸體, 赤裸** — The state or condition of being naked; nudity; bareness; defenselessness; undisguisedness.
3. **淒涼的氣氛, 黯淡** — a bleak and desolate atmosphere
*“the nakedness of the landscape”*
4. characterized by an attitude of ready accessibility (especially about one's actions or purposes); without concealment; not secretive

<a id="entry-n-000084"></a>
## nakedwood

*n.* 1. **蛇紋樹** — any of several small to medium-sized trees of Florida and West Indies with thin scaly bark and heavy dark heartwood

<a id="entry-n-000101"></a>
## name

*n.* 1. **名, 名字** — a language unit by which a person or thing is known
*“his name really is George Washington”*
*“those are two names for the same thing”*
2. **名聲, 名譽** — a person's reputation
*“he wanted to protect his good name”*
3. family based on male descent
*“he had no sons and there was no one to carry on his name”*
4. a well-known or notable person
*“they studied all the great names in the history of France”*
*“she is an important figure in modern music”*
5. by the sanction or authority of
*“halt in the name of the law”*
6. a defamatory or abusive word or phrase
*v.* 1. **取名, 命名, 稱為, 叫** — assign a specified (usually proper) proper name to
*“They named their son David”*
*“The new school was named after the famous Civil Rights leader”*
2. **列舉, 命名** — give the name or identifying characteristics of; refer to by name or some other identifying characteristic property
*“Many senators were named in connection with the scandal”*
*“The almanac identifies the auspicious months”*
3. **提名, 任命** — charge with a function; charge to be
*“She was named Head of the Committee”*
*“She was made president of the club”*
4. **分配, 分派, 委派, 提名, 任命** — create and charge with a task or function
*“nominate a committee”*
5. **提, 提及, 言及, 指, 提到** — make reference to
*“His name was mentioned in connection with the invention”*
6. mention and identify by name
*“name your accomplices!”*

<a id="entry-n-000102"></a>
## name after

*v.* 1. **與...同名** — To give (someone or something) the name of another person, place, or thing; to name (someone or something) in honour of

<a id="entry-n-000103"></a>
## name and address

*n.* 1. **地址** — written directions for finding some location; written on letters or packages that are to be delivered to that location

<a id="entry-n-000104"></a>
## name and shame

*v.* 1. *(idiomatic, transitive)* **點名批評 /點名批評** — To publish the name of (a person or organization that has committed some transgression) so as to single them out for individual blame and censure.

*n.* 1. **名牌** — A trademark, or a distinctive name for a brand, or for a range of products by a single manufacturer.
2. **名牌產品 /名牌產品** — A product having such a mark or name.

2. the feast day of a saint whose name one bears

## nap

5. sleeping for a short period of time (usually not in bed)
6. a card game similar to whist; usually played for stakes

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **自戀 /自戀** — A self-centered personality trait in which the person is infatuated with their own characteristics and image that are idealized while others and their differences are devalued in comparison (unless sameness kindles spectacular affinity that forms a rerouting of one's self-love, i.e. the person can see themselves in the other).

*adj.* 1. **狹窄+的, 窄+的** — not wide
*“a narrow bridge”*

*“a brilliant but narrow-minded judge”*
*“narrow opinions”*
3. **窄** — Having a small width; not wide; having opposite edges or sides that are close, especially by comparison to length or depth.

*“won by a narrow margin”*

5. limited in size or scope
*“the narrow sense of a word”*
6. characterized by painstaking care and detailed examination
*“a minute inspection of the grounds”*
*“a narrow scrutiny”*
*v.* 1. **專門從事, 專門化, 特化, 特殊化** — become more focus on an area of activity or field of study
*“She specializes in Near Eastern history”*
2. make or become more narrow or restricted
*“The selection was narrowed”*
*“The road narrowed”*
3. define clearly
*“I cannot narrow down the rules for this game”*
4. become tight or as if tight
*“Her throat constricted”*

## nation

**發音：** /ˈneɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **國, 國土, 國家, 州, 共和國** — a politically organized body of people under a single government
*“the state has elected a new president”*
*“African nations”*
2. *(metonymically)* **國家 /國家** — A sovereign state; (loosely, metonymic, proscribed) a country.
3. the people who live in a nation or country
*“a statement that sums up the nation's mood”*
*“the news was announced to the nation”*
4. United States prohibitionist who raided saloons and destroyed bottles of liquor with a hatchet (1846-1911)
5. a federation of tribes (especially Native American tribes)
*“the Shawnee nation”*

<a id="entry-n-000303"></a>
## nation-state

*n.* 1. **民族國家 /民族國家** — A political entity (a state) associated with a common national identity (historically, culturally, or ethnically; as with a nation) and a sovereign territorial unit.

<a id="entry-n-000304"></a>
## national

**發音：** /ˈnaʃ.ə.nəl/
*adj.* 1. **全國性+的, 國家+的** — of or relating to or belonging to a nation or country
*“national hero”*
*“national anthem”*
2. **全民性+的, 民族+的** — characteristic of or peculiar to the people of a nation
*“a national trait”*
3. **國家的 /國家的, 全國性 /全國性, 國民的 /國民的, 國立的 /國立的** — Pertaining to a nation or country, especially as a whole; affecting, shared by, or existing throughout all of a nation; the state.
4. **民族+的** — of or relating to nationality
*“national origin”*
5. limited to or in the interests of a particular nation
*“national interests”*
*“isolationism is a strictly national policy”*
6. concerned with or applicable to or belonging to an entire nation or country
*“the national government”*
*“national elections”*
*n.* 1. a person who owes allegiance to that nation
*“a monarch has a duty to his subjects”*

<a id="entry-n-000316"></a>

*“they rode the same wave of popular patriotism”*
*“British nationalism was in the air and patriotic sentiments ran high”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **民族主義 /民族主義, 國民主義 /國民主義** — A more extreme form of patriotism; the idea of a more extreme support for one's country, people or culture.
3. the doctrine that your national culture and interests are superior to any other
4. the aspiration for national independence felt by people under foreign domination
5. the doctrine that nations should act independently (rather than collectively) to attain their goals

## native

**發音：** /ˈneɪtɪv/
*n.* 1. **當地人 /當地人, 本地人** — A person who is native to a place; a person who was born in a place.
2. **土著, 土著人, 原住民** — A person of aboriginal descent, as distinguished from a person who was or whose ancestors were foreigners or settlers/colonizers. Alternative letter-case form of Native (aboriginal inhabitant of the Americas or Australia).
3. an indigenous person who was born in a particular place
*“the art of the natives of the northwest coast”*
*“the Canadian government scrapped plans to tax the grants to aboriginal college students”*
4. a person born in a particular place or country
*“he is a native of Brazil”*
5. indigenous plants and animals
*adj.* 1. **土人+的, 本國+的** — belonging to one by birth
*“my native land”*
*“one's native language”*
2. **土著** — Characteristic of or relating to people inhabiting a region from prehistoric times.
3. **本地** — Born or grown in the region in which it lives or is found; not foreign or imported.
4. **本地** — Which occurs of its own accord in a given locality, to be contrasted with a species introduced by humans.
5. characteristic of or existing by virtue of geographic origin
*“the native North American sugar maple”*
*“many native artists studied abroad”*
6. characteristic of or relating to people inhabiting a region from the beginning
*“native Americans”*
*“the aboriginal peoples of Australia”*

## natural

**發音：** /ˈnæt͡ʃ.(ə.)ɹəl/
*adj.* 1. **自然的** — Existing in the nature of a person or thing; innate, not acquired or learned.
2. **天生+的, 本能+的, 自然+的, 天然+的** — unthinking; prompted by (or as if by) instinct
*“a cat's natural aversion to water”*
*“offering to help was as instinctive as breathing”*
3. **原料狀態的, 天然+的, 未加工的, 未經加工的** — (used especially of commodities) being unprocessed or manufactured using only simple or minimal processes
*“natural yogurt”*
*“natural produce”*
4. in accordance with nature; relating to or concerning nature
*“a very natural development”*
*“our natural environment”*
5. existing in or produced by nature; not artificial or imitation
*“a natural pearl”*
*“natural gas”*
6. existing in or in conformity with nature or the observable world; neither supernatural nor magical
*“a perfectly natural explanation”*
*n.* 1. someone regarded as certain to succeed
*“he's a natural for the job”*
2. a notation cancelling a previous sharp or flat
3. (craps) a first roll of 7 or 11 that immediately wins the stake

*“the action of natural forces”*
*“volcanic activity”*

<a id="entry-n-000353"></a>

*“natural childbirth is considered the safest for the baby”*

<a id="entry-n-000354"></a>
## natural depression

*n.* 1. **凹陷地** — a sunken or depressed geological formation

<a id="entry-n-000355"></a>
## natural disaster

*n.* 1. **自然災害 /自然災害** — Any natural phenomenon that causes great damage and loss of life.
2. **自然災害 /自然災害** — The emergency situation that is the consequence of such an event.

<a id="entry-n-000356"></a>
## natural economy

*n.* 1. **自然經濟 /自然經濟** — An economy in which money is not used in the transfer of resources among people.

<a id="entry-n-000357"></a>
## natural gas

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **天然氣 /天然氣, 天然煤氣 /天然煤氣** — A mixture of gaseous hydrocarbons associated with petroleum deposits; mostly methane with smaller amounts of ethane, propane and butane; principally used as a fuel.
2. a fossil fuel in the gaseous state; used for cooking and heating homes

<a id="entry-n-000358"></a>
## natural glass

*n.* 1. **天然玻璃, 火山玻璃** — magma of any composition that cooled very rapidly

<a id="entry-n-000359"></a>
## natural history

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **博物學 /博物學** — The study of all living things, especially their origins, evolution and interrelationships.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **動植物研究 /動植物研究** — The study of all natural phenomena: botany, zoology, mineralogy, etc.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **博物學論著 /博物學論著, 動植物研究論著 /動植物研究論著** — A treatise or similar work that summarizes the known facts of either of the above.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **自然發展過程 /自然發展過程** — The progression of a medical condition in an individual.
5. the scientific study of plants or animals (more observational than experimental) usually published in popular magazines rather than in academic journals

<a id="entry-n-000360"></a>
## natural immunity

*n.* 1. **天然免疫, 自然免疫力** — immunity to disease that occurs as part of an individual's natural biologic makeup

<a id="entry-n-000361"></a>
## natural language

*n.* 1. *(countable, retronym, uncountable)* **自然語言 /自然語言** — Any human communication signal system that has evolved organically in a community into a language system and has become culturally generalized.
2. **自然語言** — a human written or spoken language used by a community; opposed to e.g. a computer language

<a id="entry-n-000362"></a>
## natural language processing

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **自然語言處理 /自然語言處理** — A field of computer science and linguistics concerned with the interactions between computers and human (natural) languages, especially computational analysis and processing of large amounts of natural language data.
2. the branch of information science that deals with natural language information

<a id="entry-n-000363"></a>
## natural language understanding

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **自然語言理解 /自然語言理解** — A subtopic of natural language processing in artificial intelligence that deals with machine reading comprehension.

<a id="entry-n-000364"></a>
## natural law

*n.* 1. **自然法, 自然法則** — a rule or body of rules of conduct inherent in human nature and essential to or binding upon human society
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **自然法** — The set of universal legal or moral principles said to be discernible from nature by reason alone; one of these principles.

<a id="entry-n-000365"></a>
## natural light

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **自然光** — Light as supplied by nature (nonmanmade light sources), such as (especially) the Sun or (sometimes) the Moon and the stars.

<a id="entry-n-000366"></a>

2. a logarithm to the base e

2. **自然數** — the number 1 and any other number obtained by adding 1 to it repeatedly

<a id="entry-n-000370"></a>

*“his favorite subject was physics”*

<a id="entry-n-000371"></a>

*“the action of natural forces”*
*“volcanic activity”*

## natural resin

*n.* 1. **自然資源** — resources (actual and potential) supplied by nature
2. **資源 /資源, 自然資源 /自然資源** — Any source of wealth that occurs naturally, especially surface water, groundwater, minerals, fossil fuels, timber, wild game, soil quality, and air quality.

<a id="entry-n-000374"></a>
## natural resources

*n.* 1. **自然資源** — resources (actual and potential) supplied by nature

<a id="entry-n-000375"></a>

<a id="entry-n-000376"></a>
## natural science

*n.* 1. **自然科學 /自然科學** — Any science involved in studying phenomena or laws of the physical world: physics, chemistry, biology, astronomy, and so on.
2. the sciences involved in the study of the physical world and its phenomena

<a id="entry-n-000377"></a>
## natural selection

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **自然選擇 /自然選擇, 天擇 /天擇** — A process by which heritable traits conferring survival and reproductive advantage to individuals, or related individuals, tend to be passed on to succeeding generations and become more frequent in a population, whereas other less favourable traits tend to become eliminated; the differential survival and reproduction of phenotypes.
2. **最適者生存, 自然淘汰, 自然選擇** — a natural process resulting in the evolution of organisms best adapted to the environment

<a id="entry-n-000378"></a>
## natural shape

*n.* 1. **自然面** — a shape created by natural forces; not man-made

<a id="entry-n-000379"></a>
## natural spring

*n.* 1. **噴泉, 泉, 泉水, 源泉** — a natural flow of ground water

<a id="entry-n-000380"></a>
## natural state

*n.* 1. **原野, 天然** — a wild primitive state untouched by civilization
*“he lived in the wild”*
*“they collected mushrooms in the wild”*

<a id="entry-n-000381"></a>
## naturalise

*v.* 1. **使歸化, 使適應新環境, 使順應, 移植, 適應新環境** — adopt to another place
*“The stories had become naturalized into an American setting”*
2. **使去除加工痕跡, 使真切自然, 使自然化, 促使符合於自然** — make more natural or lifelike
3. **使入國籍** — make into a citizen
*“The French family was naturalized last year”*
4. **使適應環境, 馴化, 馴服, 使歸化** — adapt (a wild plant or unclaimed land) to the environment
*“domesticate oats”*
*“tame the soil”*

<a id="entry-n-000382"></a>
## naturalist

**發音：** /ˈnæ.tʃə.ɹə.lɪst/
*n.* 1. *(obsolete)* **自然學家 /自然學家** — A natural philosopher; a scientist.
2. an advocate of the doctrine that the world can be understood in scientific terms
3. a biologist knowledgeable about natural history (especially botany and zoology)

<a id="entry-n-000383"></a>
## naturalistic

*adj.* 1. **寫實主義+的, 現實主義+的, 自然主義+的, 現實+的, 自然+的** — representing what is real; not abstract or ideal
*“realistic portraiture”*
*“a realistic novel”*

<a id="entry-n-000384"></a>
## naturalization

**發音：** /ˌnæ.tʃə.ɹə.laɪˈzeɪ.ʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **入籍** — The action of naturalizing somebody; act of granting citizenship.
2. the quality of being brought into conformity with nature
3. the proceeding whereby a foreigner is granted citizenship
4. the introduction of animals or plants to places where they flourish but are not indigenous
5. changing the pronunciation of a borrowed word to agree with the borrowers' phonology
*“the naturalization in English of many Italian words”*

<a id="entry-n-000385"></a>
## naturalize

**發音：** /ˈnæt͡ʃəɹəˌlaɪz/
*v.* 1. **使入國籍** — make into a citizen
*“The French family was naturalized last year”*
2. *(transitive)* **歸化, 入籍** — To grant citizenship to someone not born a citizen.
3. **使歸化, 使適應新環境, 使順應, 移植, 適應新環境** — adopt to another place
*“The stories had become naturalized into an American setting”*
4. **使去除加工痕跡, 使真切自然, 使自然化, 促使符合於自然** — make more natural or lifelike
5. **使適應環境, 馴化, 馴服, 使歸化** — adapt (a wild plant or unclaimed land) to the environment
*“domesticate oats”*
*“tame the soil”*
6. explain with reference to nature

<a id="entry-n-000386"></a>
## nature

*n.* 1. **本性, 本質** — the essential qualities or characteristics by which something is recognized
*“it is the nature of fire to burn”*
*“the true nature of jealousy”*
2. **自然** — a causal agent creating and controlling things in the universe
*“the laws of nature”*
*“nature has seen to it that men are stronger than women”*
3. **物質界, 自然界** — the natural physical world including plants and animals and landscapes etc.
*“they tried to preserve nature as they found it”*
4. the complex of emotional and intellectual attributes that determine a person's characteristic actions and reactions
*“it is his nature to help others”*
5. a particular type of thing
*“problems of this type are very difficult to solve”*
*“he's interested in trains and things of that nature”*

## near

**發音：** /nɪə/
*adj.* 1. **近+的, 靠近+的** — not far distant in time or space or degree or circumstances
*“near neighbors”*
*“in the near future”*
2. **近** — Physically close.
3. **靠左+的, 左邊+的** — being on the left side
*“the near or nigh horse is the one on the left”*
*“the animal's left side is its near or nigh side”*
4. **近似+的, 接近+的** — very close in resemblance
*“sketched in an approximate likeness”*
*“a near likeness”*
5. closely resembling the genuine article
*“near beer”*
*“a dress of near satin”*
6. giving or spending with reluctance
*“our cheeseparing administration”*
*“very close (or near) with his money”*
*adv.* 1. **接近+地, 近+地, 靠近+地** — near in time or place or relationship
*“as the wedding day drew near”*
*“stood near the door”*
2. **近** — At or towards a position close in space or time.
3. (of actions or states) slightly short of or not quite accomplished; all but
*“the job is (just) about done”*
*“the baby was almost asleep when the alarm sounded”*
*v.* 1. **臨近, 走近, 靠近, 接近** — move towards
*“We were approaching our destination”*
*“They are drawing near”*
*prep.* 1. **靠近, 接近, 在……旁邊 /在……旁邊** — Physically close to, in close proximity to.

<a id="entry-n-000498"></a>
## necessarily

**發音：** /ˈnɛs.ə.sə.ɹɪ.li/

*“such expenses are necessarily incurred”*
2. **必定+地, 必然+地** — as a highly likely consequence
*“we are necessarily bound for federalism in Europe”*
3. **必然地, 必定地** — Inevitably; of necessity.
4. in such a manner as could not be otherwise
*“it is necessarily so”*
*“we must needs by objective”*

<a id="entry-n-000499"></a>
## necessary

2. **勢必+的, 必然+的, 不可避免+的** — unavoidably determined by prior circumstances
*“the necessary consequences of one's actions”*
3. **必要的** — Required, essential, whether logically inescapable or needed in order to achieve a desired result or avoid some penalty.
*n.* 1. **必需品, 要素, 需要** — anything indispensable
*“food and shelter are necessities of life”*
*“the essentials of the good life”*

## neck and neck

*“the election was a nip and tuck affair”*
*adv.* 1. **不分勝負, 不相上下, 交頭接耳+地, 勢均力敵, 實力相當+地** — even or close in a race or competition or comparison
*“the horses ran neck and neck”*
*“he won nip and tuck”*
*phr.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **並駕齊驅 /並駕齊驅, 勢均力敵 /勢均力敵, 不相伯仲, 不相上下, 不分軒輊 /不分軒輊, 旗鼓相當 /旗鼓相當, 平分秋色** — Very close in progress, as in a race or a contest.

## need

**發音：** /niːd/
*v.* 1. **要求, 必要, 要, 需, 需求** — require as useful, just, or proper
*“It takes nerve to do what she did”*
*“success usually requires hard work”*
2. **必要, 必須, 需, 要, 需要** — have need of
*“This piano wants the attention of a competent tuner”*
3. *(transitive)* **需要** — To have an absolute requirement for.
4. *(transitive)* **需要, 要** — To want strongly; to feel that one must have something.
5. *(modal)* **必要, 必需, 得** — To be obliged or required (to do something).
6. have or feel a need for
*“always needing friends and money”*
*n.* 1. **必要, 需求, 需要** — a condition requiring relief
*“she satisfied his need for affection”*
*“God has no need of men to accomplish His work”*
2. **動機, 推動力** — the psychological feature that arouses an organism to action toward a desired goal; the reason for the action; that which gives purpose and direction to behavior
*“we did not understand his motivation”*
*“he acted with the best of motives”*
3. **貧窮, 赤貧, 缺乏, 貧困** — a state of extreme poverty or destitution
*“their indigence appalled him”*
*“a general state of need exists among the homeless”*
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **需求** — A requirement for something; something needed.
5. anything that is necessary but lacking
*“he had sufficient means to meet his simple needs”*
*“I tried to supply his wants”*

2. **忽略, 忽視** — give little or no attention to
*“Disregard the errors”*

*“How could I miss that typo?”*

5. fail to attend to

3. **漏做, 忽視, 疏忽** — the trait of neglecting responsibilities and lacking concern

5. failure to act with the prudence that a reasonable person would exercise under the same circumstances

## negotiate

**發音：** /nəˈɡəʊ.ʃi.eɪt/
*v.* 1. *(intransitive)* **談判 /談判, 協商 /協商, 交涉** — To confer with others in order to come to terms or reach an agreement.
2. **成功地繞過, 成功地越過, 順利地通過** — succeed in passing through, around, or over
*“The hiker negociated the high mountain pass”*
3. discuss the terms of an arrangement
*“They negotiated the sale of the house”*

<a id="entry-n-000589"></a>
## negotiation

**發音：** /nɪˌɡəʊ.ʃiˈeɪ.ʃən/
*n.* 1. **交涉, 會談, 談判** — a discussion intended to produce an agreement
*“the buyout negotiation lasted several days”*
*“they disagreed but kept an open dialogue”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **談判 /談判, 交涉** — The process of achieving agreement through discussion.
3. the activity or business of negotiating an agreement; coming to terms

## neighbor

*n.* 1. **鄰居** — a person who lives (or is located) near another
2. a nearby object of the same kind
*“Fort Worth is a neighbor of Dallas”*
*“what is the closest neighbor to the Earth?”*
*v.* 1. **住在鄰近, 毗鄰, 毗鄰而居** — live or be located as a neighbor
*“the neighboring house”*
2. **鄰接, 鄰近** — be located near or adjacent to
*“Pakistan neighbors India”*

<a id="entry-n-000599"></a>
## neighborhood

**發音：** /ˈneɪbə.hʊd/
*n.* 1. **周圍, 附近, 周邊** — a surrounding or nearby region
*“the plane crashed in the vicinity of Asheville”*
*“it is a rugged locality”*
2. *(US, countable, uncountable)* **附近, 鄰裡, 周圍 /周圍, 街坊** — The residential area near one's home.
3. *(US, collective, countable)* **鄰裡鄉親 /鄰里鄉親** — The inhabitants of a residential area.
4. people living near one another
*“it is a friendly neighborhood”*
*“my neighborhood voted for Bush”*
5. the approximate amount of something (usually used prepositionally as in `in the region of')
*“it was going to take in the region of two or three months to finish the job”*
*“the price is in the neighborhood of $100”*
6. an area within a city or town that has some distinctive features (especially one forming a community)
*“an ethnic neighborhood”*

## net

*adj.* 1. remaining after all deductions
*“net profit”*
2. conclusive in a process or progression
*“the final answer”*
*“a last resort”*
*n.* 1. **互聯網, 因特網, 網絡空間, 虛擬空間, 賽博空間** — a computer network consisting of a worldwide network of computer networks that use the TCP/IP network protocols to facilitate data transmission and exchange
2. **淨利, 利潤, 純利, 純利潤, 利益** — the excess of revenues over outlays in a given period of time (including depreciation and other non-cash expenses)
3. **球網** — game equipment consisting of a strip of netting dividing the playing area in tennis or badminton
4. a trap made of netting to catch fish or birds or insects
5. a goal lined with netting (as in soccer or hockey)
6. an open fabric of string or rope or wire woven together at regular intervals
*v.* 1. **淨利, 淨餘, 淨賺** — make as a net profit
*“The company cleared $1 million”*
2. **淨餘, 淨賺, 淨利** — yield as a net profit
*“This sale netted me $1 million”*
3. construct or form a web, as if by weaving
4. catch with a net
*“net a fish”*

*“the networks compete to broadcast important sports events”*
3. *(Internet)* **人脈 /人脈, 網絡 /網絡, 網路 /網路** — A computer network: multiple computers and other devices connected together to share information.
4. **網 /網** — A fabric or structure of fibrous elements attached to each other at regular intervals; the act or process of making such an object.

*“a neutral personality that made no impression whatever”*

## neutralize

**發音：** /ˈnjuːtɹəlaɪz/
*v.* 1. **中立化, 使中立** — make politically neutral and thus inoffensive
*“The treaty neutralized the small republic”*
2. *(transitive)* **中和, 抵消** — To make (something) even, inactive or ineffective.
3. **中和, 使無效, 抵銷, 使平衡, 補償** — oppose and mitigate the effects of by contrary actions
*“This will counteract the foolish actions of my colleagues”*
4. make ineffective by counterbalancing the effect of
*“Her optimism neutralizes his gloom”*
*“This action will negate the effect of my efforts”*
5. get rid of (someone who may be a threat) by killing
*“The mafia liquidated the informer”*
*“the double agent was neutralized”*
6. make incapable of military action

2. an elementary particle with zero charge and zero mass

<a id="entry-n-000882"></a>

<a id="entry-n-000883"></a>

*“they now have a new leaders”*

*“it looks like new”*

*“experiences new to him”*

## news

**發音：** /njuːz/
*n.* 1. **情報, 報導, 新聞, 消息** — information about recent and important events
*“they awaited news of the outcome”*
2. **新聞** — information reported in a newspaper or news magazine
*“the news of my death was greatly exaggerated”*
3. **新聞節目** — a program devoted to current events, often using interviews and commentary
*“we watch the 7 o'clock news every night”*
4. *(uncountable)* **消息, 新聞 /新聞** — New information of interest.
5. *(uncountable)* **新聞 /新聞** — Information about current events disseminated by the media.
6. informal information of any kind that is not previously known to someone
*“it was news to me”*

<a id="entry-n-000995"></a>
## news agency

2. an agency to collects news reports for newspapers and distributes it electronically

<a id="entry-n-000998"></a>
## news cycle

2. newspapers and magazines collectively

*“we watch the 7 o'clock news every night”*

*“he read his newspaper at breakfast”*

## Newspeak

<a id="entry-n-001014"></a>
## newspeak

*“the welfare state brought its own newspeak”*

<a id="entry-n-001015"></a>
## newsperson

*“they used bales of newspaper every day”*

**發音：** /ˈnjuːzɹuːm/

2. the staff of a newspaper or the news department of a periodical
*“every newspaper editor is criticized by the newsroom”*
3. an office in which news is processed by a newspaper or news agency or television or radio station

<a id="entry-n-001020"></a>
## Newton

**發音：** /ˈn(j)uːtən/
*prop. n.* 1. **牛頓 /牛頓** — A small village and civil parish in South Cambridgeshire district, Cambridgeshire (OS grid ref TL4349).

<a id="entry-n-001021"></a>
## newton

**發音：** /ˈn(j)uːtən/
*n.* 1. **牛頓 /牛頓** — In the International System of Units, the derived unit of force; the force required to accelerate a mass of one kilogram by one metre per second per second.
2. **牛頓** — a unit of force equal to the force that imparts an acceleration of 1 m/sec/sec to a mass of 1 kilogram; equal to 100,000 dynes
3. English mathematician and physicist; remembered for developing the calculus and for his law of gravitation and his three laws of motion (1642-1727)

<a id="entry-n-001022"></a>
## Newton's first law

*prop. n.* 1. **Newton第一定律, 牛頓第一定律 /牛頓第一定律** — The observation that objects in motion tend to stay in motion, and objects at rest tend to stay at rest unless an outside force acts upon them.

<a id="entry-n-001023"></a>
## Newton's second law

*prop. n.* 1. **Newton第二定律, 牛頓第二定律 /牛頓第二定律** — Newton's observation that the rate of change of the momentum of a body is directly proportional to, and in the same direction as, the net force acting on it. Often stated as F = ma: F = force, m = mass, a = acceleration.

<a id="entry-n-001024"></a>
## Newton's third law

*prop. n.* 1. **Newton第三定律, 牛頓第三定律 /牛頓第三定律** — Newton's observation that to every action (force applied) there is an equal and opposite reaction (equal force applied in the opposite direction).

<a id="entry-n-001025"></a>
## newtonian

*adj.* 1. **牛頓+的** — of or relating to or inspired by Sir Isaac Newton or his science
*“Newtonian physics”*
*n.* 1. a follower of Isaac Newton

<a id="entry-n-001026"></a>
## Newtown

**發音：** /ˈnjuːtaʊn/
*prop. n.* 1. **紐敦 /紐敦** — A suburb of Huntingdon, Huntingdonshire district, Cambridgeshire (OS grid ref TL2472).

<a id="entry-n-001027"></a>
## next

**發音：** /nɛkst/
*adj.* 1. **毗鄰+的, 隔壁的, 靠近+的, 鄰近+的, 鄰接+的** — nearest in space or position; immediately adjoining without intervening space
*“had adjacent rooms”*
*“in the next room”*
2. *(not-comparable, obsolete)* **下** — Most direct, or shortest or nearest in distance or time.
3. *(not-comparable)* **下一個 /下一個, 下, 下一次** — Nearest in order, succession, or rank; immediately following (or sometimes preceding) in order.
4. *(not-comparable)* **隔壁, 比鄰 /比鄰** — Nearest in relationship. (See also next of kin.)
5. immediately following in time or order
*“the following day”*
*“next in line”*
6. (of elected officers) elected but not yet serving
*“our next president”*
*adv.* 1. at the time or occasion immediately following
*“next the doctor examined his back”*

<a id="entry-n-001028"></a>
## next door

*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **隔壁** — In an adjacent building, room or place.
2. at or in or to the adjacent residence
*“the criminal had been living next door all this time”*
*adj.* 1. *(postpositional, usually)* **隔壁** — That is in an adjacent place; that is located next door.

<a id="entry-n-001029"></a>
## next of kin

*n.* 1. **直系親屬 /直系親屬, 最近親屬 /最近親屬** — Closest blood relative, heir to inheritance.
2. **直系親屬 /直系親屬, 最近親屬 /最近親屬** — Close friend, spouse, or partner (for example on a hospital admission form), one of someone's close ones.
3. the person who is (or persons who are) most closely related to a given person

<a id="entry-n-001030"></a>

## nibble

**發音：** /ˈnɪbl̩/

*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **輕咬 /輕咬** — To lightly bite (a person or animal, or part of their body), especially in a loving or playful manner; to nip.
2. *(intransitive)* **一點一點地吃 /一點一點地吃** — Chiefly followed by at, away, or on: to take a small, quick bite, or several of such bites; to eat (at frequent intervals) with small, quick bites.
3. bite off very small pieces
*“She nibbled on her cracker”*
4. bite gently
*“The woman tenderly nibbled at her baby's ear”*

*“He pieced at the sandwich all morning”*

*“so squeamish he would only touch the toilet handle with his elbow”*

**發音：** /naɪ/
*v.* 1. *(ambitransitive)* **靠近, 接近** — to draw nigh (to); to approach; to come near
*adj.* 1. **近+的, 靠近+的** — not far distant in time or space or degree or circumstances
*“near neighbors”*
*“in the near future”*
2. **靠左+的, 左邊+的** — being on the left side
*“the near or nigh horse is the one on the left”*
*“the animal's left side is its near or nigh side”*
*adv.* 1. **接近+地, 近+地, 靠近+地** — near in time or place or relationship
*“as the wedding day drew near”*
*“stood near the door”*

*“the job is (just) about done”*
*“the baby was almost asleep when the alarm sounded”*

<a id="entry-n-001129"></a>

## nip

*v.* 1. **捏, 掐, 撮** — squeeze tightly between the fingers
*“He pinched her behind”*
*“She squeezed the bottle”*
2. give a small sharp bite to
*“The Queen's corgis always nip at her staff's ankles”*
3. sever or remove by pinching or snipping
*“nip off the flowers”*
*n.* 1. **一杯, 尤指一杯酒** — a small drink of liquor
*“he poured a shot of whiskey”*
2. **滋味, 氣味, 味道** — the taste experience when a savoury condiment is taken into the mouth
3. (offensive slang) offensive term for a person of Japanese descent
4. the property of being moderately cold
*“the chilliness of early morning”*
5. a tart spicy quality
6. a small sharp bite or snip

<a id="entry-n-001263"></a>
## nip and tuck

*n.* 1. plastic surgery to remove wrinkles and other signs of aging from your face; an incision is made near the hair line and skin is pulled back and excess tissue is excised
*“some actresses have more than one face lift”*
*adj.* 1. inconclusive as to outcome; close or just even in a race or comparison or competition
*“as they approached the finish line they were neck and neck”*
*“the election was a nip and tuck affair”*
*adv.* 1. **不分勝負, 不相上下, 交頭接耳+地, 勢均力敵, 實力相當+地** — even or close in a race or competition or comparison
*“the horses ran neck and neck”*
*“he won nip and tuck”*

<a id="entry-n-001303"></a>
## nitrogen

**發音：** /ˈnaɪ.tɹə.d͡ʒən/
*n.* 1. **氮** — a common nonmetallic element that is normally a colorless odorless tasteless inert diatomic gas; constitutes 78 percent of the atmosphere by volume; a constituent of all living tissues
2. *(uncountable)* **氮, 氮氣 /氮氣** — The chemical element (symbol N) with an atomic number of 7 and atomic weight of 14.0067. It is a colorless and odorless gas.

<a id="entry-n-001304"></a>
## nitrogen balance

*n.* 1. **氮平衡** — the balance between the amount of nitrogen taken in (to the soil or the body) and the amount given off (lost or excreted)

<a id="entry-n-001305"></a>
## nitrogen cycle

*n.* 1. **氮循環** — the circulation of nitrogen; nitrates from the soil are absorbed by plants which are eaten by animals that die and decay returning the nitrogen back to the soil

<a id="entry-n-001306"></a>
## nitrogen dioxide

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **二氧化氮** — The binary compound NO₂, a brownish gas that is a poisonous air pollutant; it exists in equilibrium with dinitrogen tetroxide. It can be the poisonous result of a nitric acid spill contacting ambient air.
2. **二氧化氮** — a highly poisonous brown gas (NO2)

<a id="entry-n-001307"></a>
## nitrogen fixation

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **固氮作用** — The conversion of atmospheric nitrogen into ammonia and organic derivatives, by natural means, especially such conversion, by microorganisms in the soil, into a form that can be assimilated by plants.
2. **固氮** — the assimilation of atmospheric nitrogen by soil bacteria and its release for plant use on the death of the bacteria

<a id="entry-n-001308"></a>
## nitrogen mustard

*n.* 1. **氮芥** — Any of a series of compounds, analogous to mustard gas with sulfur replaced by nitrogen, that have been used in the treatment of cancer.
2. a toxic compound resembling mustard gas in structure; important in cancer treatment

<a id="entry-n-001309"></a>
## nitrogen oxide

*n.* 1. **氧化氮, 氮的氧化物** — any of several oxides of nitrogen formed by the action of nitric acid on oxidizable materials; present in car exhausts

<a id="entry-n-001310"></a>
## nitrogen trichloride

*n.* 1. **三氯化氮** — a yellow pungent volatile oil (trade name Agene) formerly used for bleaching and aging flour

*adj.* 1. **含氮+的** — of or relating to or containing nitrogen

<a id="entry-n-001313"></a>
## nitroglycerin

*n.* 1. **三硝酸甘油, 硝化甘油, 硝酸甘油** — a heavy yellow poisonous oily explosive liquid obtained by nitrating glycerol; used in making explosives and medically as a vasodilator (trade names Nitrospan and Nitrostat)

<a id="entry-n-001314"></a>
## nitroglycerine

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **硝化甘油** — The compound glyceryl-tri-nitrate or 1,2,3 tri-nitrooxy propane; the ester of glycerol with nitric acid; prepared by the careful addition of a mixture of nitric and sulphuric acids to glycerol with constant stirring and cooling; it is a thick, pale yellow liquid, that is highly explosive on concussion or on exposure to sudden heat; it is used in medicine as a vasodilator, and as an explosive in the form of dynamite which is safe to handle.
2. **三硝酸甘油, 硝化甘油, 硝酸甘油** — a heavy yellow poisonous oily explosive liquid obtained by nitrating glycerol; used in making explosives and medically as a vasodilator (trade names Nitrospan and Nitrostat)

<a id="entry-n-001315"></a>
## nitrohydrochloric acid

*n.* 1. **王水** — a yellow fuming corrosive mixture of nitric and hydrochloric acid that dissolves metals (including gold)

<a id="entry-n-001316"></a>
## nitrospan

*n.* 1. **三硝酸甘油, 硝化甘油, 硝酸甘油** — a heavy yellow poisonous oily explosive liquid obtained by nitrating glycerol; used in making explosives and medically as a vasodilator (trade names Nitrospan and Nitrostat)
2. trade names for nitroglycerin used as a coronary vasodilator in the treatment of angina pectoris

<a id="entry-n-001317"></a>
## nitrostat

*n.* 1. **三硝酸甘油, 硝化甘油, 硝酸甘油** — a heavy yellow poisonous oily explosive liquid obtained by nitrating glycerol; used in making explosives and medically as a vasodilator (trade names Nitrospan and Nitrostat)
2. trade names for nitroglycerin used as a coronary vasodilator in the treatment of angina pectoris

2. **亞硝酸** — an unstable inorganic acid known only in solution and as nitrite salts

## no

**發音：** /nəʊ/
*adj.* 1. **沒, 沒有+的** — quantifier; used with either mass nouns or plural count nouns for indicating a complete or almost complete lack or zero quantity of
*“we have no bananas”*
*“no eggs left and no money to buy any”*
*adv.* 1. referring to the degree to which a certain quality is present
*“he was no heavier than a child”*
2. not in any degree or manner; not at all
*“he is no better today”*
3. used to express refusal or denial or disagreement etc or especially to emphasize a negative statement
*“no, you are wrong”*
*n.* 1. **鍩** — a radioactive transuranic element synthesized by bombarding curium with carbon ions; 7 isotopes are known
2. a negative
*“his no was loud and clear”*
*det.* 1. **沒有 /沒有, 沒 /沒, 無 /無, 無有 /無有** — Not any.
2. **不是** — Not (a); not properly, not really; not fully.
*part.* 1. **不, 否, 沒 /沒, 沒有 /沒有** — Used to show disagreement, negation, denial, refusal, or prohibition.

*n.* 1. **稀有氣體 /稀有氣體, 惰性氣體 /惰性氣體** — Any of the elements of group 18 of the periodic table, being monatomic, usually inert (with very limited exceptions), and gaseous at familiar temperatures.
2. **惰性氣體** — any of the chemically inert gaseous elements of the helium group in the periodic table

<a id="entry-n-001396"></a>
## noble metal

*n.* 1. **抗腐蝕金屬 /抗腐蝕金屬, 惰性金屬 /惰性金屬** — A metal resistant to corrosion, oxidizing and tarnishing, such as gold or platinum.
2. **貴金屬** — any metal that is resistant to corrosion or oxidation

<a id="entry-n-001397"></a>
## noble savage

<a id="entry-n-001398"></a>

2. a titled peer of the realm

<a id="entry-n-001399"></a>
## noblesse

2. members of the nobility (especially of the French nobility)

<a id="entry-n-001400"></a>

**發音：** /nəʊˌblɛs ɒˈbliːʒ/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **貴族義務 /貴族義務** — The honourable obligation that is the responsibility of those of high rank; in American English this often includes the expectation of benevolent actions such as helping those less fortunate.
2. the obligation of those of high rank to be honorable and generous (often used ironically)

<a id="entry-n-001401"></a>
## noblewoman

2. a woman of the peerage in Britain

<a id="entry-n-001412"></a>
## nocturnal

**發音：** /nɒkˈtɜː.nəl/
*adj.* 1. **夜的, 晝伏夜出 /晝伏夜出** — Of a person, creature, group, or species, primarily active during the night.
2. **夜間的 /夜間的** — Of an occurrence, taking place at night, nightly.
3. **夜+的, 夜間+的** — of or relating to or occurring in the night
*“nocturnal darkness”*
4. belonging to or active during the night
*“nocturnal animals are active at night”*
*“nocturnal plants have flowers that open at night and close by day”*

<a id="entry-n-001415"></a>
## nod

**發音：** /nɒd/
*v.* 1. **點頭** — lower and raise the head, as to indicate assent or agreement or confirmation
*“The teacher nodded when the student gave the right answer”*
2. *(ambitransitive)* **點頭 /點頭** — To incline the head up and down, as to indicate agreement.
3. *(intransitive)* **打盹** — To gradually fall asleep.
4. express or signify by nodding
*“He nodded his approval”*
5. let the head fall forward through drowsiness
*“The old man was nodding in his chair”*
6. sway gently back and forth, as in a nodding motion
*“the flowers were nodding in the breeze”*
*n.* 1. **點頭 /點頭** — An instance of inclining the head up and down, as to indicate agreement, or as a cursory greeting.
2. a sign of assent or salutation or command
3. the act of nodding the head

<a id="entry-n-001416"></a>

2. change from a waking to a sleeping state
*“he always falls asleep during lectures”*

4. **結節, 瘤, 節** — any thickened enlargement
5. **節點 /節點** — A leaf node.
6. **網絡節點 /網絡節點, 節點 /節點** — A computer or other device attached to a network.

## noise

**發音：** /ˈnoɪ̯z/
*n.* 1. **噪音** — sound of any kind (especially unintelligible or dissonant sound)
*“he enjoyed the street noises”*
*“they heard indistinct noises of people talking”*
2. *(uncountable)* **噪音, 響聲 /響聲** — Various sounds, usually unwanted or unpleasant.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **噪音** — Any part of a signal or data that reduces the clarity, precision, or quality of the desired output.
4. **無序性, 隨機性** — the quality of lacking any predictable order or plan
5. the auditory experience of sound that lacks musical quality; sound that is a disagreeable auditory experience
*“modern music is just noise to me”*
6. electrical or acoustic activity that can disturb communication
*v.* 1. **製造噪音, 發出嘈雜聲, 吵鬧, 喧鬧** — emit a noise

<a id="entry-n-001432"></a>
## noise cancellation

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **降噪** — The active cancellation of outside noise by creation of an inverse waveform to null out noise soundwaves within the subject environment.

<a id="entry-n-001433"></a>

*n.* 1. **噪聲** — the condition of being noisy (as in a communication channel)

<a id="entry-n-001434"></a>
## noise pollution

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **噪音汙染** — Excess noise which is unpleasant or harmful.
2. **噪聲汙染, 噪音汙染** — annoying and potentially harmful environmental noise

<a id="entry-n-001435"></a>
## noiselessly

*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **無聲地 /無聲地** — In a quiet manner, without any noise.
2. without a sound
*“he stood up soundlessly and speechlessly and glided across the hallway and through a door”*

<a id="entry-n-001436"></a>

*“he blew his nose noisily”*

<a id="entry-n-001454"></a>
## nominal

*adj.* 1. **寫有名字+的, 含名字+的** — relating to or constituting or bearing or giving a name
*“the Russian system of nominal brevity”*
*“a nominal lists of priests”*
2. **名詞+的, 起名詞作用+的** — pertaining to a noun or to a word group that functions as a noun
*“nominal phrase”*
*“noun phrase”*
3. **名義上+的, 有名無實+的** — of, relating to, or characteristic of an amount that is not adjusted for inflation
*“the nominal GDP”*
*“nominal interest rates”*
4. insignificantly small; a matter of form only (`tokenish' is informal)
*“the fee was nominal”*
*“a token gesture of resistance”*
5. named; bearing the name of a specific person
*“nominative shares of stock”*
6. existing in name only
*“the nominal (or titular) head of his party”*
*n.* 1. a phrase that can function as the subject or object of a verb

**發音：** /ˈnɒmɪnəlaɪz/

<a id="entry-n-001460"></a>
## nominate

**發音：** /ˈnɒm.ɪ.neɪt/
*v.* 1. **提名, 提出** — propose as a candidate for some honor
2. **提名, 指名** — To name someone as a candidate for a particular role or position, including that of an office.
3. **任命, 提名** — put forward; nominate for appointment to an office or for an honor or position
*“The President nominated her as head of the Civil Rights Commission”*
4. **提名, 任命** — charge with a function; charge to be
*“She was named Head of the Committee”*
*“She was made president of the club”*
5. **分配, 分派, 委派, 提名, 任命** — create and charge with a task or function
*“nominate a committee”*

<a id="entry-n-001461"></a>
## nomination

*n.* 1. **提名** — the act of officially naming a candidate
*“the Republican nomination for Governor”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **提名, 指名** — An act or instance of nominating.
3. **被任命, 被提名** — the condition of having been proposed as a suitable candidate for appointment or election
*“there was keen competition for the nomination”*
*“his nomination was hotly protested”*
4. an address (usually at a political convention) proposing the name of a candidate to run for election
*“the nomination was brief and to the point”*

<a id="entry-n-001696"></a>
## norm

**發音：** /nɔːm/
*n.* 1. **基準** — a standard or model or pattern regarded as typical
*“the current middle-class norm of two children per family”*
2. **基準, 平均數** — a statistic describing the location of a distribution
*“it set the norm for American homes”*
3. **常態 /常態, 規範 /規範** — That which is normal or typical.

<a id="entry-n-001697"></a>
## Norma

*prop. n.* 1. **矩尺座** — An inconspicuous constellation of the southern sky, said to resemble a carpenter's square. It lies south of the constellations Scorpius and Centaurus.

<a id="entry-n-001698"></a>
## normal

**發音：** /ˈnɔː.məl/
*adj.* 1. **標準+的, 正常+的, 通常+的, 非異常+的, 平常+的** — conforming with or constituting a norm or standard or level or type or social norm; not abnormal
*“serve wine at normal room temperature”*
*“normal diplomatic relations”*
2. **正常, 通常, 常態 /常態** — According to norms or rules or to a regular pattern.
3. in accordance with scientific laws
4. being approximately average or within certain limits in e.g. intelligence and development
*“a perfectly normal child”*
*“of normal intelligence”*
5. forming a right angle
*n.* 1. *(countable)* **法線 /法線** — A line or vector that is perpendicular to another line, surface, or plane.
2. **習俗, 標準, 準則, 方案, 慣例** — something regarded as a normative example
*“the convention of not naming the main character”*
*“violence is the rule not the exception”*

<a id="entry-n-001699"></a>
## normal distribution

**發音：** /ˌnɔɹməl ˌdɪstɹəˈbjuʃən/
*n.* 1. **正態分佈 /正態分佈, 常態分佈 /常態分佈** — Any of a family of continuous probability distributions such that the probability density function is the Gaussian function
2. a theoretical distribution with finite mean and variance

<a id="entry-n-001700"></a>
## normal saline

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **生理鹽水 /生理鹽水** — A solution of 0.9% w/v of NaCl, about 300 mOsm/L.

<a id="entry-n-001701"></a>
## normal school

*n.* 1. *(dated)* **師範學校 /師範學校** — A school for training teachers, especially in mainland Europe and North America.
2. a two-year school for training elementary teachers

<a id="entry-n-001702"></a>
## normalcy

**發音：** /ˈnɔɹməlsi/
*n.* 1. **常態, 正常狀態, 標準** — being within certain limits that define the range of normal functioning
2. *(US, countable, uncountable)* **正常, 常態 /常態, 正規 /正規** — The state of being normal; the fact of being normal; normality.
3. expectedness as a consequence of being usual or regular or common

<a id="entry-n-001703"></a>
## normality

**發音：** /nɔːˈmæl.ɪ.ti/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **正常, 常態 /常態, 正規 /正規** — The state of being normal or usual; normalcy.
2. **常態, 正常狀態, 標準** — being within certain limits that define the range of normal functioning
3. **當量濃度, 規定濃度, 規度** — (of a solution) concentration expressed in gram equivalents of solute per liter
4. expectedness as a consequence of being usual or regular or common

<a id="entry-n-001704"></a>

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **正常化, 正規化 /正規化** — Any process that makes something more normal or regular, which typically means conforming to some regularity or rule, or returning from some state of abnormality.

*“a committee was appointed to recommend terminological standardization”*

## nose

*n.* 1. **鼻子** — the organ of smell and entrance to the respiratory tract; the prominent part of the face of man or other mammals
*“he has a cold in the nose”*
2. **很短的距離** — a small distance
*“my horse lost the race by a nose”*
3. a front that resembles a human nose (especially the front of an aircraft)
*“the nose of the rocket heated up on reentry”*
4. the front or forward projection of a tool or weapon
*“he ducked under the nose of the gun”*
5. a symbol of inquisitiveness
*“keep your nose out of it”*
6. the sense of smell (especially in animals)
*“the hound has a good nose”*
*v.* 1. **干涉, 探出, 探聽, 刺探, 干預** — search or inquire in a meddlesome way
*“This guy is always nosing around the office”*
2. **嗅, 嗅到, 聞, 聞到, 嗅出** — catch the scent of; get wind of
*“The dog nosed out the drugs”*
3. advance the forward part of with caution
*“She nosed the car into the left lane”*
4. push or move with the nose
5. rub noses
6. defeat by a narrow margin

**發音：** /ˈnəʊtəbl̩/
*adj.* 1. **值得注意+的, 值得注目+的, 顯著+的** — worthy of notice
*“a noteworthy advance in cancer research”*
2. **著名** — Worthy of note; remarkable; memorable; noted or distinguished.
3. **值得注意的** — Easily noted (without connotations of value); clearly noticeable, conspicuous.
4. widely known and esteemed
*“a famous actor”*
*“a celebrated musician”*
*n.* 1. **名人, 名士** — A person or thing of distinction.
2. a celebrity who is an inspiration to others
*“he was host to a large gathering of luminaries”*

<a id="entry-n-001879"></a>
## notarize

*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **公證 /公證** — To be witness of the authenticity of a document and its accompanying signatures in one's capacity as notary public.
2. authenticate as a notary
*“We had to have the signature notarized”*

<a id="entry-n-001880"></a>
## notary

**發音：** /ˈnoʊtəɹi/
*n.* 1. *(Canada, US)* **公證人 /公證人, 公證員 /公證員** — A notary public, a public officer who serves as an impartial witness to the signing of important documents, but who is not authorised to practise law.
2. someone legally empowered to witness signatures and certify a document's validity and to take depositions

<a id="entry-n-001881"></a>
## notary public

**發音：** /ˈnəʊtəɹi ˈpʌblɪk/
*n.* 1. **公證人 /公證人, 公證員 /公證員** — An officer who can administer oaths and statutory declarations, witness and authenticate documents and perform certain other acts varying from jurisdiction to jurisdiction.
2. someone legally empowered to witness signatures and certify a document's validity and to take depositions

<a id="entry-n-001882"></a>
## notation

**發音：** /noʊˈteɪʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **記法 /記法, 表示法** — A system of characters, symbols, or abbreviated expressions used in an art or science or in mathematics or logic to express technical facts or quantities.
2. **註釋, 註解** — a comment or instruction (usually added)
*“his notes were appended at the end of the article”*
*“he added a short notation to the address on the envelope”*
3. *(countable)* **記號 /記號, 注釋 /註釋** — A specific note or piece of information written in such a notation.
4. a technical system of symbols used to represent special things
5. the activity of representing something by a special system of marks or characters

<a id="entry-n-001883"></a>
## notch

**發音：** /nɒtʃ/
*v.* 1. cut or make a notch into
*“notch the rope”*
2. notch a surface to record something
*n.* 1. **V字形切口** — a V-shaped indentation
*“mandibular notch”*
2. **刻痕, 凹痕** — A V-shaped cut.
3. **刻痕** — a small cut
4. the location in a range of mountains of a geological formation that is lower than the surrounding peaks
*“we got through the pass before it started to snow”*
5. a V-shaped or U-shaped indentation carved or scratched into a surface
*“there were four notches in the handle of his revolver”*

<a id="entry-n-001884"></a>
## notched

*adj.* 1. **成鋸齒狀+的, 有鋸齒+的, 有鋸齒狀+的, 有齒+的, 鋸齒狀+的** — notched like a saw with teeth pointing toward the apex
2. **有凹口+的, 有缺口+的, 有鋸狀+的, 鋸齒+的, 有鋸齒狀+的** — having an irregularly notched or toothed margin as though gnawed

<a id="entry-n-001885"></a>
## note

**發音：** /nəʊ̯t/
*v.* 1. **提到, 注意, 評論, 說起, 談到** — make mention of
*“She observed that his presentation took up too much time”*
*“They noted that it was a fine day to go sailing”*
2. **注意到, 理會, 注意** — notice or perceive
*“She noted that someone was following her”*
*“mark my words”*
3. **觀察, 注意, 留意** — observe with care or pay close attention to
*“Take note of this chemical reaction”*
4. *(transitive)* **留心, 注意** — To notice with care; to observe; to remark; to heed.
5. *(transitive)* **記錄 /記錄** — To record in writing; to make a memorandum of.
6. *(transitive)* **註解 /註解** — To annotate.
*n.* 1. **短信** — a short personal letter
*“drop me a line when you get there”*
2. **音符** — a notation representing the pitch and duration of a musical sound
*“the singer held the note too long”*
3. **莊票, 紙鈔, 鈔票** — a piece of paper money (especially one issued by a central bank)
*“he peeled off five one-thousand-zloty notes”*
4. **註釋, 註解** — a comment or instruction (usually added)
*“his notes were appended at the end of the article”*
*“he added a short notation to the address on the envelope”*
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **音符** — A character, variously formed, to indicate the length of a tone, and variously placed upon the staff to indicate its pitch.
6. *(countable, uncountable)* **字條 /字條, 筆記 /筆記** — A brief piece of writing intended to assist the memory; a memorandum; a minute.

*“I had to co-sign his note at the bank”*

*“a noteworthy advance in cancer research”*

*“a noteworthy fact is that her students rarely complain”*
*“a remarkable achievement”*

## nothing

**發音：** /ˈnʌθɪŋ/
*n.* 1. **什麼也沒有, 虛無, 零, 無** — a quantity of no importance
*“it looked like nothing I had ever seen before”*
*“reduced to nil all the work we had done”*
*adv.* 1. in no respect; to no degree
*“he looks nothing like his father”*
*pron.* 1. *(indefinite, pronoun)* **沒什麼 /沒什麼, 沒事 /沒事** — Not any thing; no thing.

<a id="entry-n-001896"></a>
## nothingness

*n.* 1. **不存在** — the state of nonexistence
2. empty rhetoric or insincere or exaggerated talk
*“that's a lot of wind”*
*“don't give me any of that jazz”*

<a id="entry-n-001897"></a>
## notice

**發音：** /ˈnəʊ.tɪs/
*v.* 1. **找到, 探測, 覺察, 發現, 察覺** — discover or determine the existence, presence, or fact of
*“She detected high levels of lead in her drinking water”*
*“We found traces of lead in the paint”*
2. **注意到, 理會, 注意** — notice or perceive
*“She noted that someone was following her”*
*“mark my words”*
3. *(archaic, transitive)* **理會 /理會, 注意** — To remark upon; to mention.
4. *(transitive)* **注意** — To become aware of; to observe.
5. make or write a comment on
*“he commented the paper of his colleague”*
6. express recognition of the presence or existence of, or acquaintance with
*“He never acknowledges his colleagues when they run into him in the hallway”*
*“She acknowledged his complement with a smile”*
*n.* 1. **通知** — an announcement containing information about an event
*“you didn't give me enough notice”*
*“an obituary notice”*
2. **正式通知** — advance notification (usually written) of the intention to withdraw from an arrangement of contract
*“we received a notice to vacate the premises”*
*“he gave notice two months before he moved”*
3. *(uncountable)* **注意** — The act of observing; perception.
4. *(countable)* **公告, 通知, 啟事 /啟事** — A written or printed announcement.
5. *(countable)* **通知書 /通知書** — A formal notification or warning.
6. *(countable)* **評論 /評論** — A published critical review of a play or the like.

<a id="entry-n-001898"></a>
## notice board

*n.* 1. **佈告欄, 揭示板** — a board that hangs on a wall; displays announcements

<a id="entry-n-001899"></a>
## noticeable

*adj.* 1. **值得注意+的, 引人注目+的** — readily noticed
*“a noticeable resemblance”*
2. capable or worthy of being perceived
*“noticeable shadows under her eyes”*
*“noticeable for its vivid historical background”*
3. capable of being detected
*“after a noticeable pause the lecturer continued”*
4. undesirably noticeable
*“the obtrusive behavior of a spoiled child”*
*“equally obtrusive was the graffiti”*

<a id="entry-n-001900"></a>
## noticeably

*adv.* 1. **可知覺+地, 引人著目+地, 顯然+地, 看得出+地, 醒目+地** — in a noticeable manner
*“he changed noticeably over the years”*

## notification

**發音：** /ˌnəʊtɪfɪˈkeɪʃn̩/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **通知** — The act of notifying.
2. *(countable)* **通知** — A specific piece of information that serves to notify.
3. an accusation of crime made by a grand jury on its own initiative
4. informing by words
5. a request for payment
*“the notification stated the grace period and the penalties for defaulting”*

<a id="entry-n-001902"></a>
## notify

**發音：** /ˈnoʊtɪfaɪ/
*v.* 1. **使注意, 通知** — inform (somebody) of something
*“I advised him that the rent was due”*
2. *(transitive)* **通知, 通告** — To give (someone) notice (of some event).

<a id="entry-n-001903"></a>
## notion

**發音：** /ˈnəʊʃən/
*n.* 1. **想法, 構思, 理解, 觀念, 思想** — a general inclusive concept
2. **概念** — Mental apprehension of whatever may be known, thought, or imagined; idea, concept.
3. a vague idea in which some confidence is placed
*“his impression of her was favorable”*
*“what are your feelings about the crisis?”*
4. an odd or fanciful or capricious idea
*“the theatrical notion of disguise is associated with disaster in his stories”*
*“he had a whimsy about flying to the moon”*
5. (usually plural) small personal articles or clothing or sewing items
*“buttons and needles are notions”*

<a id="entry-n-001904"></a>
## notional

*adj.* 1. **假想+的, 想象+的, 想象中+的, 幻想+的, 虛構+的** — not based on fact; unreal
*“the falsehood about some fanciful secret treaties"- F.D.Roosevelt”*
*“a small child's imaginary friends”*
2. not based on fact or investigation
*“a notional figure of cost helps in determining production costs”*
*“speculative knowledge”*
3. indulging in or influenced by fancy
*“a fanciful mind”*
*“all the notional vagaries of childhood”*
4. being of the nature of a notion or concept
*“a plan abstract and conceptional”*
*“to improve notional comprehension”*

<a id="entry-n-001960"></a>
## now

**發音：** /naʊ/
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **現在 /現在, 如今, 現今 /現今, 現下 /現下, 現時 /現時, 這陣 /這陣, 這晚子 /這晚子** — At the present time.
2. in the historical present; at this point in the narration of a series of past events
*“President Kennedy now calls in the National Guard”*
*“Washington now decides to cross the Delaware”*
3. in these times
*“it is solely by their language that the upper classes nowadays are distinguished"- Nancy Mitford”*
*“we now rarely see horse-drawn vehicles on city streets”*
4. used to preface a command or reproof or request
*“now hear this!”*
*“now pay attention”*
5. at the present moment
*“goods now on sale”*
*“the now-aging dictator”*
6. without delay or hesitation; with no time intervening
*“he answered immediately”*
*“found an answer straightaway”*
*n.* 1. the momentary present
*“Now is a good time to do it”*
*“it worked up to right now”*

<a id="entry-n-001961"></a>
## now and then

**發音：** /ˌnaʊ ən(d)ˈðɛn/
*adv.* 1. *(idiomatic, not-comparable)* **偶爾 /偶爾, 時而 /時而** — Synonym of occasionally, sometimes, intermittently.
2. now and then or here and there
*“he was arrogant and occasionally callous”*
*“open areas are only occasionally interrupted by clumps of trees”*

<a id="entry-n-001962"></a>
## now that

*conj.* 1. **既然** — As a consequence of the fact that; since.

<a id="entry-n-001963"></a>
## now you're talking

*phr.* 1. *(colloquial, idiomatic)* **好啊** — A phrase indicating agreement with a previously stated suggestion to change a course of action.

<a id="entry-n-001964"></a>
## now, now

*interj.* 1. **好了，好了** — A gentle reproach or admonition.
2. **好了，好了** — A reassurance.

<a id="entry-n-001965"></a>
## nowadays

**發音：** /ˈnaʊ.ə.deɪz/
*n.* 1. **現在, 當今, 目前** — the period of time that is happening now; any continuous stretch of time including the moment of speech
*“that is enough for the present”*
*“he lives in the present with no thought of tomorrow”*
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **現在 /現在, 現今 /現今, 當今 /當今** — At the present time; in the current era.
2. in these times
*“it is solely by their language that the upper classes nowadays are distinguished"- Nancy Mitford”*
*“we now rarely see horse-drawn vehicles on city streets”*

<a id="entry-n-001966"></a>
## nowhere

**發音：** /ˈnoʊ.(h)wɛɹ/
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **無處 /無處** — In no place.
2. not anywhere; in or at or to no place
*“I am going nowhere”*
*n.* 1. **不重要的地方** — an insignificant place
*“he came out of nowhere”*

<a id="entry-n-001967"></a>
## nowise

*adv.* 1. **毫不, 決不** — in no manner
*“they are nowise different”*

<a id="entry-n-001968"></a>
## nowrooz

*n.* 1. **農曆新年假期, 波斯新年, 波斯春節, 納吾肉孜節, 諾魯茲節** — (Persian) the new year holiday in Iran and Azerbaijan and Afghanistan and Pakistan and parts of India and among the Kurds; comes at the vernal equinox

## Nowruz

**發音：** /nəʊ̯ˈɹuːz/
*prop. n.* 1. **納吾肉孜節 /納吾肉孜節, 諾魯茲節 /諾魯茲節, 諾魯孜節 /諾魯孜節, 伊朗的新年** — The New Year in the Iranian and Zoroastrian calendars, celebrated on the spring equinox.

2. **有害+的** — injurious to physical or mental health
*“noxious chemical wastes”*
*“noxious ideas”*

## nu

<a id="entry-n-001981"></a>
## nuance

**發音：** /ˈnjuː.ɑːns/
*n.* 1. **細微差別** — a subtle difference in meaning or opinion or attitude
*“without understanding the finer nuances you can't enjoy the humor”*
*“don't argue about shades of meaning”*
2. *(countable)* **細微區別 /細微區別, 小差異 /小差異, 微小差別 /微小差別** — A minor distinction.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **細膩 /細膩, 微妙** — Subtlety or fine detail.
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **添加細微差別 /添加細微差別, 微妙地改變 /微妙地改變, 細緻調整 /細緻調整** — To apply a nuance to; to change or redefine in a subtle way.

<a id="entry-n-001982"></a>
## nuanced

*adj.* 1. **具有細微差別的, 具有細微差別的, 細緻入微, 細緻入微, 精確 /精確, 複雜 /複雜, 細膩** — Having nuances; possessed of multiple layers of detail, pattern, or meaning.

## Nuannuan

*prop. n.* 1. **暖暖** — A district of Keelung, Taiwan.

## nub

2. a small piece
*“a nub of coal”*
*“a stub of a pencil”*

*“the heart and soul of the Republican Party”*

*n.* 1. **小塊, 小腫塊, 瘤, 結節** — a small lump or protuberance

*“a sweater knitted of nubbly homespun yarns”*

<a id="entry-n-001987"></a>

*adj.* 1. **粗紡+的** — of textiles; having a rough surface
*“a sweater knitted of nubbly homespun yarns”*
