*prop. n.* 1. **努比亞 /努比亞** — The region of the upper Nile valley now part of Sudan and far-southern Egypt.

<a id="entry-n-001989"></a>
## nuchal cord

*n.* 1. **臍帶繞頸 /臍帶繞頸** — A condition in which the umbilical cord becomes wrapped around the fetus's neck.

<a id="entry-n-001990"></a>
## nuchal scan

*n.* 1. **頸後透明帶掃描 /頸後透明帶掃描** — An ultrasound performed to help determine the chances that the foetus may develop chromosomal conditions such as Down syndrome.

<a id="entry-n-001991"></a>
## nuclear

**發音：** /ˈn(j)u.kli.ɚ/
*adj.* 1. **原子+的, 原子核+的, 原子武器+的, 核+的, 核能+的** — (weapons) deriving destructive energy from the release of atomic energy
*“nuclear war”*
*“nuclear weapons”*
2. **原子能+的** — of or relating to or constituting the nucleus of an atom
*“nuclear physics”*
*“nuclear fission”*
3. **原子核+的** — of or relating to or constituting the nucleus of a cell
*“nuclear membrane”*
*“nuclear division”*
4. *(not-comparable)* **核** — Involving energy released by nuclear reactions (fission, fusion, radioactive decay).
5. *(not-comparable)* **核** — Relating to a weapon that derives its force from rapid release of energy through nuclear reactions.
6. *(not-comparable)* **核** — Pertaining to the nucleus of a cell.

<a id="entry-n-001992"></a>
## nuclear bomb

*n.* 1. **核彈 /核彈, 核子彈 /核子彈** — A nuclear weapon.

<a id="entry-n-001993"></a>
## nuclear chemistry

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **核化學 /核化學** — The application of chemical techniques to the solution of problems in nuclear physics, including the discovery of naturally occurring and synthetic radioactive elements and the results of nuclear fission.
2. the chemistry of radioactive substances

<a id="entry-n-001994"></a>
## nuclear deterrent

*n.* 1. **核威懾 /核威懾** — The arsenal of nuclear weapons of a nation viewed as a deterrent to an enemy from attacking.

<a id="entry-n-001995"></a>
## nuclear energy

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **核能, 核動力 /核動力, 原子能** — The energy released by a nuclear reaction; either through nuclear fission or nuclear fusion
2. **原子能, 核能, 核動力** — the energy released by a nuclear reaction

<a id="entry-n-001996"></a>
## nuclear fallout

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **放射性落下灰** — Residual radioactive material in the upper atmosphere which settles down to Earth over a protracted period; typically ash which has been propelled upwards by a nuclear explosion or disaster, sometimes mixed with rain or snow.

<a id="entry-n-001997"></a>
## nuclear family

*n.* 1. **核心家庭** — The traditional western family unit, consisting of two parents (traditionally a mother and a father) and their children.
2. a family consisting of parents and their children and grandparents of a marital partner

<a id="entry-n-001998"></a>
## nuclear fission

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **核裂變 /核裂變, 核分裂** — A nuclear reaction in which a large nucleus splits into smaller ones, sometimes accompanied by the simultaneous release of energy.
2. **核分裂, 核裂變** — a nuclear reaction in which a massive nucleus splits into smaller nuclei with the simultaneous release of energy

<a id="entry-n-001999"></a>
## nuclear force

*n.* 1. **核力** — The force that acts between nucleons and binds protons and neutrons into atomic nuclei; the residual strong force.

<a id="entry-n-002000"></a>
## nuclear fuel

*n.* 1. **核燃料** — A radioactive material, such as uranium, used to power a nuclear reactor.
2. **原子核燃料, 核子燃料** — fuel (such as uranium) that can be used in nuclear reactors as a source of electricity

<a id="entry-n-002001"></a>
## nuclear fusion

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **核聚變 /核聚變, 核融合** — The combining of the nuclei of atoms to form the nuclei of larger ones, with a resulting release of large quantities of energy; the process that makes the sun shine, and hydrogen bombs explode.
2. **核子融合, 核聚變, 核融合, 聚變** — a nuclear reaction in which nuclei combine to form more massive nuclei with the simultaneous release of energy

<a id="entry-n-002002"></a>
## nuclear fusion reaction

*n.* 1. **核子融合, 核聚變, 核融合, 聚變** — a nuclear reaction in which nuclei combine to form more massive nuclei with the simultaneous release of energy

<a id="entry-n-002003"></a>
## nuclear magnetic resonance

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **核磁共振** — The absorption of electromagnetic radiation (radio waves), at a specific frequency, by an atomic nucleus placed in a strong magnetic field; used in spectroscopy and in magnetic resonance imaging.
2. resonance of protons to radiation in a magnetic field

<a id="entry-n-002004"></a>
## nuclear matrix

*n.* 1. **核基質 /核基質** — A framework of fibers extending throughout the nucleus of a cell.

<a id="entry-n-002005"></a>
## nuclear medicine

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **核醫學 /核醫學** — The branch of medicine that uses radioactive isotopes in the diagnosis and treatment of disease.
2. the branch of medicine that uses radioactive materials either to image a patient's body or to destroy diseased cells

<a id="entry-n-002006"></a>
## nuclear meltdown

*n.* 1. **堆芯熔燬, 熔融, 熔化** — severe overheating of the core of a nuclear reactor resulting in the core melting and radiation escaping

<a id="entry-n-002007"></a>
## nuclear membrane

*n.* 1. **核膜, 核被膜** — A double lipid bilayer that surrounds the nucleus in eukaryotic cells; it contains the genetic material (DNA).

<a id="entry-n-002008"></a>
## nuclear physics

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **原子核物理學 /原子核物理學, 核物理學 /核物理學, 核物理** — The branch of physics that studies the nucleus of the atom, its internal structure and components.
2. the branch of physics that studies the internal structure of atomic nuclei

<a id="entry-n-002009"></a>
## nuclear power

*n.* 1. **核動力, 核能** — nuclear energy regarded as a source of electricity for the power grid (for civilian use)
2. *(countable)* **核動力 /核動力, 核電 /核電, 核國家 /核國家** — A nation that possesses nuclear weapons.

<a id="entry-n-002010"></a>
## nuclear power plant

*n.* 1. **核電廠 /核電廠** — A power plant that obtains its primary energy from the heat generated in nuclear reactions.

<a id="entry-n-002011"></a>
## nuclear reaction

*n.* 1. **核反應** — (physics) a process that alters the energy or structure or composition of atomic nuclei
2. **核反應 /核反應** — A process such as the fission of an atomic nucleus, or the fusion of one or more atomic nuclei and/or subatomic particles in which the number of protons and/or neutrons in a nucleus changes; the reaction products may contain a different element or a different isotope of the same element.

<a id="entry-n-002012"></a>
## nuclear reactor

*n.* 1. **反應堆, 核反應堆** — (physics) any of several kinds of apparatus that maintain and control a nuclear reaction for the production of energy or artificial elements
2. **核反應堆 /核反應堆, 原子爐 /原子爐** — Any device in which a controlled chain reaction is maintained for the purpose of creating heat (for power generation) or for creating neutrons and other fission products for experimental, medical or other purposes.

<a id="entry-n-002013"></a>
## nuclear rna

*n.* 1. **核RNA** — ribonucleic acid found in the nucleolus of the cell

<a id="entry-n-002014"></a>
## nuclear submarine

*n.* 1. **核子動力潛艇 /核子動力潛艇, 核動力潛艇 /核動力潛艇** — A submarine powered by a nuclear reactor.
2. a submarine that is propelled by nuclear power

<a id="entry-n-002015"></a>
## nuclear triad

*n.* 1. **核三位一體 /核三位一體** — Three traditional components of a nuclear weapons arsenal: strategic bombers, intercontinental ballistic missiles, and submarine-launched ballistic missiles.

<a id="entry-n-002016"></a>
## nuclear war

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **核戰爭 /核戰爭, 核子戰爭 /核子戰爭, 核戰 /核戰** — A war fought using nuclear (fission and/or fusion) weapons.

<a id="entry-n-002017"></a>
## nuclear warfare

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **核戰爭** — The use of nuclear weapons as weapons of war.

<a id="entry-n-002018"></a>
## nuclear warhead

*n.* 1. **原子彈頭, 核彈頭, 熱核彈頭** — the warhead of a missile designed to deliver an atom bomb

<a id="entry-n-002019"></a>
## nuclear weapon

*n.* 1. **核武器, 核子武器, 核武** — A weapon that derives its energy from the nuclear reactions of either fission or fusion.
2. a weapon of mass destruction whose explosive power derives from a nuclear reaction

<a id="entry-n-002020"></a>
## nuclear winter

*n.* 1. **核冬天** — A predicted drop in global temperature following a nuclear war due to dust in the upper atmosphere reducing sunlight reaching the ground.
2. a long period of darkness and extreme cold that scientists predict would follow a full-scale nuclear war; a layer of dust and smoke in the atmosphere would cover the earth and block the rays of the sun; most living organisms would perish

<a id="entry-n-002021"></a>
## nuclear-free zone

*n.* 1. **無核區 /無核區** — An area in which the local or regional authorities have banned nuclear weapons and nuclear power stations.

<a id="entry-n-002022"></a>
## nuclease

*n.* 1. **核酸酶** — Any of several enzymes capable of cleaving the phosphodiester bonds between the nucleotide subunits of nucleic acids.
2. general term for enzymes that catalyze the hydrolysis of nucleic acid by cleaving chains of nucleotides into smaller units

<a id="entry-n-002023"></a>
## nucleate

*v.* 1. **成核** — form into a nucleus
*“Some cells had nucleated”*
*adj.* 1. **有核+的, 核形+的** — having a nucleus or occurring in the nucleus
*“nucleated cells”*

<a id="entry-n-002024"></a>
## nucleated

*adj.* 1. **有核+的, 核形+的** — having a nucleus or occurring in the nucleus
*“nucleated cells”*

<a id="entry-n-002025"></a>
## nucleic acid

**發音：** /njuːˈkliː.ɪk ˈæsɪd/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **核酸** — Any acidic, chainlike biological macromolecule consisting of multiple repeat units of phosphoric acid, sugar and purine and pyrimidine bases; they are involved in the preservation, replication and expression of hereditary information in every living cell
2. (biochemistry) any of various macromolecules composed of nucleotide chains that are vital constituents of all living cells

<a id="entry-n-002026"></a>
## nucleic acid test

*n.* 1. **核酸檢測 /核酸檢測** — A technique used to detect a particular nucleic acid sequence, usually to identify a particular organism, often a virus or bacteria.

<a id="entry-n-002027"></a>
## nucleobase

*n.* 1. **核鹼基** — The base of a nucleic acid, such as thymine, uracil, adenine, cytosine and guanine.

<a id="entry-n-002028"></a>
## nucleoid

**發音：** /ˈnjuːkliˌɔɪd/
*n.* 1. **擬核 /擬核** — The irregularly-shaped region within a prokaryote cell where the genetic material is localized

<a id="entry-n-002029"></a>
## nucleon number

*n.* 1. **核子數** — the sum of the number of neutrons and protons in an atomic nucleus

<a id="entry-n-002030"></a>
## nucleoprotein

*n.* 1. **核蛋白, 核蛋白質** — any of several substances found in the nuclei of all living cells; consists of a protein bound to a nucleic acid

<a id="entry-n-002031"></a>
## nucleoside

**發音：** /ˈn(j)u(ː)kliəˌsaɪd/
*n.* 1. **核苷** — an organic molecule in which a nitrogenous heterocyclic base (or nucleobase), which can be either a double-ringed purine or a single-ringed pyrimidine, is covalently attached to a five-carbon pentose sugar (deoxyribose in DNA or ribose in RNA). When the phosphate group is covalently attached to the pentose sugar, it forms a nucleotide.
2. a glycoside formed by partial hydrolysis of a nucleic acid

<a id="entry-n-002032"></a>
## nucleotide

**發音：** /ˈn(j)u(ː)kliəˌtaɪd/
*n.* 1. **核苷酸** — The monomer constituting DNA or RNA biopolymer molecules. Each nucleotide consists of a nitrogenous heterocyclic base (or nucleobase), which can be either a double-ringed purine or a single-ringed pyrimidine; a five-carbon pentose sugar (deoxyribose in DNA or ribose in RNA); and a phosphate group.
2. a phosphoric ester of a nucleoside; the basic structural unit of nucleic acids (DNA or RNA)

<a id="entry-n-002033"></a>
## nucleus

**發音：** /ˈnjuː.kli.əs/
*n.* 1. **核心, 核** — The core, central part of something, around which other elements are assembled.
2. **原子核** — The massive, positively charged central part of an atom, made up of protons and neutrons.
3. **細胞核 /細胞核** — A large membrane-enclosed organelle found in eukaryotic cells which contains genetic material.
4. a part of the cell containing DNA and RNA and responsible for growth and reproduction
5. the positively charged dense center of an atom
6. a small group of indispensable persons or things
*“five periodicals make up the core of their publishing program”*

<a id="entry-n-002034"></a>
## nuclide

**發音：** /ˈn(j)uːklaɪd/

*“The academy counts several Nobel Prize winners among its members”*
3. **數, 點, 算, 計數, 計算** — determine the number or amount of
*“Can you count the books on your shelf?”*
*“Count your change”*

*“We must number the names of the great mathematicians”*
6. place a limit on the number of

## nurse

**發音：** /nɜːs/
*n.* 1. **保姆, 護士, 看護, 看護者** — one skilled in caring for young children or the sick (usually under the supervision of a physician)
2. *(archaic)* **保育員 /保育員** — A person (usually a woman) who takes care of other people’s children.
3. a woman who is the custodian of children
*v.* 1. **照料, 看護, 護理, 照顧** — serve as a nurse; care for sick or handicapped people
2. **看護, 護理, 照顧** — treat carefully
*“He nursed his injured back by lying in bed several hours every afternoon”*
*“He nursed the flowers in his garden and fertilized them regularly”*
3. try to cure by special care of treatment, of an illness or injury
*“He nursed his cold with Chinese herbs”*
4. maintain (a theory, thoughts, or feelings)
*“bear a grudge”*
*“entertain interesting notions”*
5. give suck to
*“The wetnurse suckled the infant”*
*“You cannot nurse your baby in public in some places”*

<a id="entry-n-002114"></a>

2. a registered nurse who has received special training and can perform many of the duties of a physician

**發音：** /ˈnʊʃuː/
*prop. n.* 1. *(historical)* **女書 /女書** — A syllabic script which was used exclusively among the ethnic Yao women of Jiangyong, Hunan, China, from the 9th century, used to write the Xiangnan Tuhua lect of Chinese, which mostly fell out of use during the 20th century.

<a id="entry-n-002202"></a>
## Nüwa

*prop. n.* 1. *(Chinese)* **女媧 /女媧** — a goddess who repaired heaven and created the Earth

<a id="entry-n-002203"></a>
## Nārada

*prop. n.* 1. **那羅陀佛 /那羅陀佛** — The name of the 12th of 27 named Buddhas immediately preceding Gautama.
